\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\|f\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}\,\|\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}+C\delta\Bigl\|\frac{\widehat{u_{2}^{a}}}{\psi}\Bigr\|_{\infty}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\delta\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{\infty}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha-(0,0,1)}\partial_{y}G\|_{2}\,\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sigma}\left(1+\Bigl\|\frac{\widehat{u_{2}^{a}}}{\psi}\Bigr\|_{\infty}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{\infty}^{2}\right)\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{4}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}G\|_{2}^{2},

where
\widehat{u^{a}_{2}}=u_{2}^{a}-\varepsilon^{2}fe^{-y}
.

For
a_{2}
, by Hölder’s inequality and Lemma
5.1
, it shows

\displaystyle a_{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}u_{1}^{a}\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\Bigl\|\frac{\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{u_{2}^{a}}}{\psi}\Bigr\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon^{2}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}(fe^{-y})\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}G\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(1+\frac{1}{\sigma}\right)\Biggl[\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}u_{1}^{a}\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\Bigl\|\frac{\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{u_{2}^{a}}}{\psi}\Bigr\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}(fe^{-y})\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr]\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{4}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}G\|_{2}^{2}.

Combining the above estimates and by Lemma
5.3
, the proof (a) is complete.

(b) For
\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\bigl\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u\cdot\nabla G^{a}),\partial^{\alpha}Q\bigr\rangle\bigr|
, by Hölder’s inequality and
\widehat{u_{2}}=u_{2}-\varepsilon^{2}fe^{-y}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\bigl\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u\cdot\nabla G^{a}),\partial^{\alpha}Q\bigr\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\bigl|\bigl\langle\partial^{\alpha-\beta}u\cdot\partial^{\beta}\nabla G^{a},\partial^{\alpha}Q\bigr\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq C\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\|\partial^{\alpha-\beta}u_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\,\|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x}G^{a}\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}Q\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\Bigl\|\frac{\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\widehat{u_{2}}}{\psi}\Bigr\|_{2}^{2}\,\|\psi\partial^{\beta}\partial_{y}G^{a}\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}Q\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{2}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\|\partial^{\alpha-\beta}(fe^{-y})\|_{\infty}^{2}\,\|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{y}G^{a}\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}Q\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}},

and by Lemma
5.1
, this becomes

\displaystyle\leq C_{\delta}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}u_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left\|\frac{\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{u_{2}}}{\psi}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m+1}\|\partial^{\alpha}G^{a}\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}Q\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{2}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}(fe^{-y})\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}G^{a}\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}Q\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Through Lemma
5.3
, Lemma
5.4
and the definition (
2.15
) we know that

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}u_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left\|\frac{\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{u_{2}}}{\psi}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|u_{1}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|u_{2}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}u_{2}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|u_{1}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|u_{2}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}u_{2}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right).

Then by using Hölder’s inequality we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\bigl\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u\cdot\nabla G^{a}),\partial^{\alpha}Q\bigr\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)\|\partial^{\alpha}G^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}_{\infty}}\|Q\|_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m}_{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}G^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|Q\|_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left(\|G^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}_{\infty}}^{2}+1\right)\|Q\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\left(\|G^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}G^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right).

(c) For
\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u\cdot\nabla G),\partial^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|
, we divide it into two situations:

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u\cdot\nabla G),\partial^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha}u\cdot\nabla G,\partial^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta\leq\alpha,1\leq|\beta|\end{subarray}}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha-\beta}u\cdot\partial^{\beta}\nabla G,\partial^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=:c_{1}+c_{2},

For
c_{1}
, by Hölder’s inequality, Lemma
5.2
and
\widehat{u_{2}}=u_{2}-\varepsilon^{2}fe^{-y}
, we have

\displaystyle c_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}u_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\left\|\partial_{x}G\right\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left\|\frac{\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{u}_{2}}{\psi}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\psi\partial_{y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{2}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}(fe^{-y})\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}u_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left\|\frac{\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{u}_{2}}{\psi}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\times

\displaystyle\quad\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq 2}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq 2}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{2}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}(fe^{-y})\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

and by Lemma
5.3
and Lemma
5.4
, this becomes

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}u\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\omega\|_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m+1}\|\partial^{\alpha}(fe^{-y})\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq 2}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq 2}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon^{2}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}(fe^{-y})\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}\right)\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\partial_{y}G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\partial_{y}G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m}_{\infty}}\|\nabla G\|_{Y^{1,m}}\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}.

For
c_{2}
, using the same methods as Hölder’s inequality, Lemma
5.3
and Lemma
5.4
, we have

\displaystyle c_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m-1}\|\partial^{\alpha}u\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}u\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}u\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}\right)\|\nabla G\|_{Y^{1,m}}\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}+C\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}\|\nabla G\|_{Y^{1,m}}\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}.

In summary, by Hölder’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u\cdot\nabla G),\partial^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)+\frac{C}{\sigma\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla G\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\frac{C}{\sigma}\varepsilon^{2}\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\frac{C}{\sigma^{2}\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{2}\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\frac{C}{\sigma}\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m}_{\infty}}^{2}\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sigma}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)+\frac{C}{\sigma\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C}{\sigma}\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\left\|G\right\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla G\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

MM

\square

Lemma 5.6
.

For
H\nabla G
, we have

(a)

\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\bigl\langle\partial^{\alpha}(H^{a}\nabla G),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\bigr\rangle\bigr|\leq C\|H^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}_{\infty}}\|\nabla G\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla Q\|_{Y^{1,m}},

(5.86)

(b)

\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\bigl\langle\partial^{\alpha}(H\nabla G^{a}),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\bigr\rangle\bigr|\leq C\|H\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla G^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}_{\infty}}\|\nabla Q\|_{Y^{1,m}},

(5.87)

(c)

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\bigl\langle\partial^{\alpha}(H\nabla G),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\bigr\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq C\|H\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla H\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla G\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla Q\|_{Y^{1,m}}

(5.88)

\displaystyle+\|H\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla G\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}G\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla Q\|_{Y^{1,m}},

and

(d)
While
\partial_{y}Q|_{y=0}=0
, it shows that

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\bigl\langle\partial^{\alpha}(H\nabla G),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\bigr\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq C\|H\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla H\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla G\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla Q\|_{Y^{1,m}}

(5.89)

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|H\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|G\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla G\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla Q\|_{Y^{1}}+\|\nabla^{2}Q\|_{Y^{1,m}}\right).

Proof.

(a) By Hölder’s inequality, direct computations derive that

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(H^{a}\nabla G),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha-\beta}H^{a}\partial^{\beta}\nabla G,\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}H^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

and (b) is similar.

(c) The term
\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(H\nabla G),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\rangle\right|

can be divided into two parts, one case is at least one derivative falls on
\nabla G
(denoted
c_{1}
) and the other is all derivatives fall on
H
(denoted
c_{2}
):

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(H\nabla G),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta\leq\alpha\\
1\leq|\beta|\end{subarray}}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha-\beta}H\,\partial^{\beta}\nabla G,\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\rangle\right|+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha}H\,\nabla G,\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=:c_{1}+c_{2}.

By Lemma
5.2
we have

\displaystyle c_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m-1}\|\partial^{\alpha}H\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{\!\!\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\!\!\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\!\!\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}H\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\!\!\frac{1}{4}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}H\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\!\!\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\!\!\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\!\!\frac{1}{2}}

and

\displaystyle c_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}H\|_{2}^{2}\|\nabla G\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{\!\!\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\!\!\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}H\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\!\!\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\tau\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq 1}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\!\!\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\sum_{\tau\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq 1}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla^{2}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\!\!\frac{1}{4}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\!\!\frac{1}{2}}.

Combining
c_{1}
and
c_{2}
, (c) is proved.

(d) Note that

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(H\nabla G),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta\leq\alpha,1\leq|\beta|\end{subarray}}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha-\beta}H\partial^{\beta}\nabla G,\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\rangle\right|+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha}H\nabla G,\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\quad=:d_{1}+d_{2}.

The estimate of
d_{1}
is similar to
c_{1}
in (c), thus we only need to consider
d_{2}
. By Hölder’s inequality and Lemma
5.2
, it shows

\displaystyle d_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha}H\partial_{x}G,\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x}Q\rangle\right|+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha}H\partial_{y}G,\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}Q\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}H\|_{2}^{2}\left\|\partial_{x}G\right\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x}Q\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}H\|_{2}^{2}\|\psi\partial_{y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left\|\frac{\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}Q}{\psi}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

and by Lemma
5.2
and Lemma
5.3
, this becomes

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}H\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\partial_{x}G\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x}Q\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\delta}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}H\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\psi\partial_{y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}\left(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{y}Q\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla^{2}Q\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\delta}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}H\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\tau\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq 2}\|\partial^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\sum_{\tau\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq 2}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla Q\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla^{2}Q\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Combining
d_{1}
and
d_{2}
, the proof of (d) is complete.

MM

\square

Lemma 5.7
.

For
\nabla H\cdot\nabla G
, we have

(a)

\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\bigl\langle\partial^{\alpha}((\nabla H^{a}\cdot\nabla)G),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\bigr\rangle\bigr|\leq C\|\nabla H^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}_{\infty}}\|\nabla G\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla Q\|_{Y^{1,m}}.

(5.90)

(b)

\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\bigl\langle\partial^{\alpha}((\nabla H\cdot\nabla)G^{a}),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\bigr\rangle\bigr|\leq C\|\nabla G^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}_{\infty}}\|\nabla H\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla Q\|_{Y^{1,m}}.

(5.91)

(c)
While
\partial_{y}Q|_{y=0}=0
, it shows that

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\bigl\langle\partial^{\alpha}((\nabla H\cdot\nabla)G),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\bigr\rangle\bigr|

(5.92)

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla H\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla G\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}G\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla Q\|_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|H\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla H\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla G\|_{Y^{1,m}}\left(\|\nabla Q\|_{Y^{1}}+\|\nabla^{2}Q\|_{Y^{1,m}}\right).

Proof.

The proof of (a) and (b) is similar to the cases of Lemma
5.6
.

For term (c), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(\nabla H\cdot\nabla)G,\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta\leq\alpha,|\beta|\leq m-1\end{subarray}}\left|\langle\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\nabla H\cdot\partial^{\beta}\nabla G,\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\nabla H\cdot\partial^{\alpha}\nabla G,\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=:e_{1}+e_{2}.

For
e_{1}
, by Hölder inequality and Lemma
5.2
, it shows

\displaystyle e_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla H\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\tau\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m-1}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla G\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla H\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla^{2}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\times

\displaystyle\quad\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

The estimate for
e_{2}
follows a similar strategy, but requires Lemma
5.3
to handle
\partial_{y}Q
:

\displaystyle e_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial_{x}H\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla G\|_{2}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x}Q\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\psi\partial_{y}H\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla G\|_{2}\left\|\frac{\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}Q}{\psi}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\delta}\left(\sum_{\tau\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq 2}\|\partial^{\alpha}H\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\sum_{\tau\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq 2}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}H\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\times

\displaystyle\quad\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla Q\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla^{2}Q\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Combining
e_{1}
and
e_{2}
, this proof can be proven.

MM

\square

5.3.
The proof of conormal Sobolev-type estimates

Next, we perform the energy estimate, which essentially involves taking
\partial^{\alpha}
on both sides of the equation, forming the
L^{2}
-inner product with
\partial^{\alpha}G
(where
G
corresponds to the principal term of the equation), and then summing over
\alpha
.

Proof of Proposition
3.1
.

First, apply
\partial^{\alpha}
to both sides of the first equation of (
2.11
) and take the inner product with
\partial^{\alpha}n
, this gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}n\|_{2}^{2}-\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Delta n,\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\left|\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla n+u\cdot\nabla n^{a}+u\cdot\nabla n),\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}(\nabla\cdot(n^{a}\nabla c+n\nabla c^{a}+n\nabla c)),\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}N,\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=:A_{1}+A_{2}+A_{3}.

Then using parts by integration, we get

\displaystyle-\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Delta n,\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial_{x}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x}n,\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle-\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}n,\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad-\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle[\partial^{\alpha},\partial_{y}]\partial_{y}n,\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x}n,\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x}n\rangle+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}n,\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}n\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad-\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}n,[\partial_{y},\partial^{\alpha}]n\rangle-\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle[\partial^{\alpha},\partial_{y}]\partial_{y}n,\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle.

For the third term or the last term, it is sufficient to consider
\alpha_{3}\neq 0
(other cases vanish),
and we have

\displaystyle-\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Delta n,\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla n\|_{2}^{2}-C\delta\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}n,\partial^{\alpha-(0,0,1)}\partial_{y}n\rangle|

(5.93)

\displaystyle\geq(1-C\delta)\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla n\|_{2}^{2}.

For
A_{1}
, we divide it into three parts:

\displaystyle A_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla n),\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle|+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u\cdot\nabla n^{a}),\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u\cdot\nabla n),\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle|

\displaystyle=:A_{11}+A_{12}+A_{13}.

For
A_{11}
, by Lemma
5.5
(a), it shows

\displaystyle A_{11}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma}\left(1+\|u^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}_{\infty}}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m}_{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{2}\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{4}\|\partial_{y}n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

For
A_{12}
, by Lemma
5.5
(b), it follows that

\displaystyle A_{12}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left(\|n^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}_{\infty}}^{2}+1\right)\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\left(\|n^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}n^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right).

For
A_{13}
, by Lemma
5.5
(c), we have

\displaystyle A_{13}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sigma}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)+\frac{C}{\sigma\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C}{\sigma}\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

Summing up, we get

\displaystyle A_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma}\left(1+\|n^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}_{\infty}}+\|u^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}_{\infty}}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m}_{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{2}\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

(5.94)

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\left(\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|n^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}n^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\sigma}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)+\frac{C}{\sigma\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma}\left(1+\|n^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}_{\infty}}+\|u^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}_{\infty}}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)^{2}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\left(\|n^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}n^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\sigma\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}.

For
A_{2}
, we have the following estimate:

\displaystyle A_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(n^{a}\nabla c+n\nabla c^{a}+n\nabla c),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla n\rangle|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle[\partial^{\alpha},\partial_{y}](n^{a}\partial_{y}c+n\partial_{y}c^{a}+n\partial_{y}c),\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(n^{a}\partial_{y}c+n\partial_{y}c^{a}+n\partial_{y}c),[\partial_{y},\partial^{\alpha}]n\rangle|,

by (
2.17
) and Hölder inequality, which becomes

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(n^{a}\nabla c+n\nabla c^{a}+n\nabla c),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla n\rangle|

\displaystyle\quad+C\delta\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(n^{a}\partial_{y}c+n\partial_{y}c^{a}+n\partial_{y}c),\partial^{\alpha-(0,0,1)}\partial_{y}n\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(n^{a}\nabla c),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla n\rangle|+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(n^{a}\partial_{y}c),\partial^{\alpha-(0,0,1)}\partial_{y}n\rangle|\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(n\nabla c^{a}),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla n\rangle|+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(n\partial_{y}c^{a}),\partial^{\alpha-(0,0,1)}\partial_{y}n\rangle|\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(n\nabla c),\partial^{\alpha}\nabla n\rangle|+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(n\partial_{y}c),\partial^{\alpha-(0,0,1)}\partial_{y}n\rangle|\right)

\displaystyle=:A_{21}+A_{22}+A_{23}.

For
A_{21}
, by Lemma
5.6
(a), we have

\displaystyle A_{21}

\displaystyle\leq C\|n^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sigma}\|n^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

For
A_{22}
, by Lemma
5.6
(b), it shows

\displaystyle A_{22}

\displaystyle\leq C\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sigma}\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}^{2}\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

For
A_{23}
, by Lemma
5.6
(c), this becomes

\displaystyle A_{23}

\displaystyle\leq\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}+\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sigma\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\frac{C}{\sigma}\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\frac{C}{\sigma}\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,1}}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sigma\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\frac{C}{\sigma^{3}}\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{2}+\frac{C}{\sigma^{3}\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{2}.

Summing up, we get

\displaystyle A_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\sigma}\|n^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\frac{C}{\sigma}\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}^{2}\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

(5.95)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\sigma^{3}}\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\sigma^{3}\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\sigma}\left(1+\|n^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{2}\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\sigma^{3}\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}+C\sigma\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{2}.

For
A_{3}
, using the Young inequality, we have

\displaystyle\biggl|\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m,\;\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\alpha}N,\;\partial^{\alpha}n\rangle\biggr|

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m,\;\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\alpha}N\|_{2}^{2}+C\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m,\;\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\alpha}n\|_{2}^{2}

(5.96)

\displaystyle\leq C\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\|N\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

Summing up, one can prove Proposition
3.1
by combining (
5.93
), (
5.94
), (
5.95
) and (
5.96
).

MM

\square

Proof of Proposition
3.2
.

Applying
\partial^{\tau}_{t}
to both sides of the second equation of (
2.11
) and taking the inner product with
\partial^{\tau}_{t}c
, this gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\|_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\Delta c,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla c+u\cdot\nabla c+u\cdot\nabla c^{a}),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(c^{a}n+cn^{a}+cn),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}K,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle=:B_{1}+B_{2}+B_{3}.

On the left, by integration by parts, we have

\displaystyle-\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\Delta c,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\rangle=\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla c\|_{2}^{2}.

(5.97)

On the right, for
B_{1}
, we divide it into three parts:

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla c+u\cdot\nabla c+u\cdot\nabla c^{a}),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla c),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\rangle\bigr|+C\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u\cdot\nabla c^{a}),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\rangle\bigr|+C\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u\cdot\nabla c),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle=:B_{11}+B_{12}+B_{13}.

For the first and second terms, by applying the Hölder inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle C\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla c),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\rangle\bigr|\leq C\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u^{a}\|_{\infty}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla c\|_{2}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\|_{2}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle C\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u\cdot\nabla c^{a}),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\rangle\bigr|\leq C\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\|_{2}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla c^{a}\|_{\infty}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\|_{2}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}.

For the remaining term, a direct application of Lemma
5.2
and Hölder inequality yields

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u\cdot\nabla c),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\|_{\infty}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla c\|_{2}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\|_{2}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\biggl(\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2,\;\alpha_{1}\leq 1}\|\partial^{\alpha}u\|_{2}\,\|\partial_{y}\partial^{\alpha}u\|_{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla c\|_{2}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\|_{2}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2,\;\alpha_{1}\leq 1}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla u\|_{2}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla c\|_{2}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\|_{2}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}.

Summing up, we get

\displaystyle B_{1}\leq

\displaystyle C\biggl(\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1}}\|c\|_{Y^{1}}+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}\|c\|_{Y^{1}}+\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}+\|\nabla u\|_{Y^{1,2}}\right)\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1}}\|c\|_{Y^{1}}\biggr)

(5.98)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(1+\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}+\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}\right)\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|c\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{C}{\sigma}\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|c\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}\right)^{3}.

For
B_{2}
, using the same methods as in
B_{13}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(c^{a}n+cn^{a}+cn),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}c^{a}\|_{\infty}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}n\|_{2}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\|_{2}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+C\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}n^{a}\|_{\infty}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\|_{2}^{2}+C\biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}n\|_{\infty}^{2}\biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\left(\|c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}+\|n^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}\right)\left(\|n\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|c\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}\right)+C\|n\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}n\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|c\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\left(1+\|c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}+\|n^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}\right)\left(\|n\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|c\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}\right)+\frac{C}{\sigma}\left(\|n\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+\|c\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}\right)^{3}+C\sigma\|\partial_{y}n\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}.

(5.99)

For
B_{3}
, by Young inequality, it shows

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}K,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}K\|_{2}^{2}+C\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}c\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|K\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+C\|c\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}.

(5.100)

Summing up, this proposition can be proved by (
5.97
), (
5.98
), (
5.3
) and (
5.3
).

MM

\square

Proof of Proposition
3.3
.

Apply
\partial^{\alpha}\nabla
to both sides of the second equation in (
2.11
) and take the inner product with
\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c
, We have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\|_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\Delta c,\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle

\displaystyle\quad\leq\Biggl|\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\left((\nabla u^{a}\cdot\nabla)c+(\nabla u\cdot\nabla)c^{a}+(\nabla u\cdot\nabla)c\right),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\Biggr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\Biggl|\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\left((u^{a}\cdot\nabla)\nabla c+(u\cdot\nabla)\nabla c^{a}+(u\cdot\nabla)\nabla c\right),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\Biggr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\Biggl|\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}(\nabla c^{a}n+\nabla cn^{a}+\nabla cn),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\Biggr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\Biggl|\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}(c^{a}\nabla n+c\nabla n^{a}+c\nabla n),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\Biggr|+\Biggl|\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\nabla K,\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\Biggr|

\displaystyle=:\bar{B}_{1}+\bar{B}_{2}+\bar{B}_{3}+\bar{B}_{4}+\bar{B}_{5}.

On the left, by integration by parts, it shows

\displaystyle-\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\Delta c,\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\partial_{x}c,\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\partial_{x}c\rangle+\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\partial_{y}c,\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\partial_{y}c\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\partial_{y}c,\;[\partial^{\alpha},\partial_{y}]\nabla c\rangle-\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle[\partial^{\alpha},\partial_{y}]\nabla\partial_{y}c,\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle,

which is greater than

\displaystyle\geq\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla^{2}c\|_{2}^{2}-C\delta\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\partial_{y}c,\;\partial^{\alpha-(0,0,1)}\partial_{y}\nabla c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\geq(1-C\delta)\,\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla^{2}c\|_{2}^{2},

(5.101)

due to (
2.17
).

Estimate of
\bar{B}_{1}
.
We divide it into three parts:

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}((\nabla u^{a}\cdot\nabla)c),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\bigr|+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}((\nabla u\cdot\nabla)c^{a}),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}((\nabla u\cdot\nabla)c),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\quad=:\bar{B}_{11}+\bar{B}_{12}+\bar{B}_{13}.

For
\bar{B}_{11}
, by Lemma
5.7
(a), it follows that

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{11}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

For
\bar{B}_{12}
, by Lemma
5.7
(b) and Lemma
5.4
, we have

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{12}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\|\nabla u\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\left(\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}\right)\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}.

For
\bar{B}_{13}
, by Lemma
5.7
(c), it shows

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{13}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla u\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\delta}\|u\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1}}+\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}\right),

and by Lemma
5.4
and Young inequality, this becomes

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)+C\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\delta}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\delta}\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)+\frac{C}{\sigma\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

In summary, we get

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{1}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\left(\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}\right)\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}

(5.102)

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)+\frac{C}{\sigma\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma}\bigr(1+\|\nabla u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}+\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}\bigl)^{2}\bigr(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\bigl)

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\left(1+\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\right)+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma^{2}\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}.

Estimate of
\bar{B}_{2}
.
Similar with
\bar{B}_{1}
, it is divided into three terms:

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}((u^{a}\cdot\nabla)\nabla c),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\bigr|+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}((u\cdot\nabla)\nabla c^{a}),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}((u\cdot\nabla)\nabla c),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle=:\bar{B}_{21}+\bar{B}_{22}+\bar{B}_{23}.

For
\bar{B}_{21}
, by Lemma
5.5
(a), we can derive

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{21}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma}\left(1+\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\right)^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{4}\|\partial_{y}\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

For
\bar{B}_{22}
, by Lemma
5.5
(b), we have

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{22}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left(\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+1\right)\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\left(\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right).

For
\bar{B}_{23}
, by Lemma
5.5
(c), it follows that

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{23}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sigma}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)+\frac{C}{\sigma\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C}{\sigma}\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

Summing up, we get

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma}\left(1+\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}+\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\right)^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

(5.103)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\sigma}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)+\frac{C}{\sigma\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\sigma}\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\left(\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma}\left(1+\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}+\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}\right)^{2}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\sigma\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\left(\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right).

Estimate of
\bar{B}_{3}
.
It splits into three terms:

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{3}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(\nabla c^{a}n),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\bigr|+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(\nabla cn^{a}),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(\nabla cn),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle=:\bar{B}_{31}+\bar{B}_{32}+\bar{B}_{33}.

For
\bar{B}_{31}
, by Lemma
5.6
(b), we obtain

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{31}

\displaystyle\leq C\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}.

For
\bar{B}_{32}
, by Lemma
5.6
(a), this yields

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{32}

\displaystyle\leq C\|n^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

For
\bar{B}_{33}
, by Lemma
5.6
(d), it shows

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{33}

\displaystyle\leq C\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\delta}\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|c\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1}}+\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}\right).

In summary, we get

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{3}\leq

\displaystyle C\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}+C\|n^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

(5.104)

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|c\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1}}+\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma}\left(1+\|\nabla c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}+\|n^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\right)\left(\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma}\left(\|n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}+C\sigma\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

Estimate of
\bar{B}_{4}
.
We can separate it into three parts:

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{4}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(c^{a}\nabla n),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\bigr|+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(c\nabla n^{a}),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(c\nabla n),\;\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle=:\bar{B}_{41}+\bar{B}_{42}+\bar{B}_{43}.

For
\bar{B}_{41}
, by Lemma
5.6
(a), we have

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{41}

\displaystyle\leq C\|c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sigma}\|c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

For
\bar{B}_{42}
, by Lemma
5.6
(b), this becomes

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{42}

\displaystyle\leq C\|c\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla n^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}.

For
\bar{B}_{43}
, by Lemma
5.6
(d), it follows that

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{43}

\displaystyle\leq C\|c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\delta}\|c\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|n\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,1}}+\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}\right),

which is controlled by

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sigma}\left(\|c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma}\left(\|c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{2}\|n\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\sigma\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{2}+\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma^{3}\varepsilon^{4}}\|c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{4}\|n\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+C\sigma\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

In summary, we get

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{4}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma}\left(1+\|c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}^{2}\right)\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\|c\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|\nabla n^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}

(5.105)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\sigma}\left(\|c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma^{3}\varepsilon^{4}}\|c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{4}\|n\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma}\left(1+\|c^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\nabla n^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}^{2}\right)\left(\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)+C\sigma\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma^{3}\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|n\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

Estimate of
\bar{B}_{5}
.
By Young inequality, this yields

\displaystyle\bar{B}_{5}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\tau\leq 1,\;|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla K\|_{2}^{2}+C\sum_{\tau\leq 1,\;|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla c\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla c\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\|\nabla K\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

(5.106)

Summing up, one can prove this proposition by (
5.3
) to (
5.3
).
MM

\square

Proof of Proposition
3.4
.

Applying
\partial^{\tau}_{t}
to both sides of the third equation in (
2.11
) and taking the inner product with
\partial^{\tau}_{t}u
, this yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\|_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\Delta u,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle

\displaystyle\quad\leq\Biggl|\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\left(u^{a}\cdot\nabla u+u\cdot\nabla u^{a}+u\cdot\nabla u\right),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle\Biggr|+\Biggl|\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla p,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle\Biggr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\Biggl|\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}n,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u_{2}\rangle\Biggr|+\Biggl|\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}U,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle\Biggr|=:I_{1}^{\prime}+\cdots+I_{4}^{\prime}

On the left hand side, by integration by parts we have

\displaystyle-\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\Delta u,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle

\displaystyle=-\varepsilon^{2}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{x}^{2}u,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle+\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{y}^{2}u,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle\Biggr)

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{2}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{x}u,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{x}u\rangle+\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{y}u,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{y}u\rangle-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\partial\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{y}u_{2}\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u_{2}dx\Biggr),

and by Gauss theorem, Hölder inequality and Young inequality, this becomes

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla u\|_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\partial\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{y}u_{2}\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}fdx

\displaystyle\geq\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla u\|_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon^{4}\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\biggl|\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\partial^{\tau}_{t}f\;\partial_{y}\left(\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{x}u_{1}e^{-y}\right)\biggr|

\displaystyle\geq\varepsilon^{2}(1-C\sigma)\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla u\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{C}{\sigma}\varepsilon^{4}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{x}fe^{-y}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}fe^{-y}\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr).

(5.107)

On the right hand side, we decompose the convective term into the following three parts:

\displaystyle I_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Biggl|\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\left(u^{a}\cdot\nabla u+u\cdot\nabla u^{a}+u\cdot\nabla u\right),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle\Biggr|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla u),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle\bigr|+C\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u\cdot\nabla u^{a}),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle\bigr|+C\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u\cdot\nabla u),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle\bigr|,

which are estimated, respectively. For the first term
u^{a}\cdot\nabla u
, by Hölder inequality, we derive

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla u),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq C\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u^{a}\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla u\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

For the second term
u\cdot\nabla u^{a}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u\cdot\nabla u^{a}),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq C\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla u^{a}\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\|_{2}^{2}.

For the last term
u\cdot\nabla u
, we perform a more refined estimate:

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u\cdot\nabla u),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle\bigr|\leq C\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla u\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}},

and by Lemma
5.2
and Lemma
5.4
, this becomes

\displaystyle\leq C\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,\;|\alpha|\leq 2}\|\partial^{\alpha}u\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{4}}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,\;|\alpha|\leq 2}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}u\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{4}}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla u\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,\;|\alpha|\leq 2}\|\partial^{\alpha}\omega\|_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,\;|\alpha|\leq 3}\|\partial^{\alpha}(fe^{-y})\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

In summary, we get

\displaystyle I_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}\|\nabla u\|_{Y^{1}}\|u\|_{Y^{1}}+C\|\nabla u^{a}\|_{Y^{1}_{\infty}}\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}

(5.108)

\displaystyle+C\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,3}}^{2}\right)\|u\|_{Y^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}+\|\nabla u^{a}\|_{Y^{1}_{\infty}}\right)\left(\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}\right)+\varepsilon^{4}\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,3}}^{2}\right)^{3}.

Finally, we estimate the pressure term, the coupling term and the external force term, leading to the following inequalities:

\displaystyle I_{2}^{\prime}\leq\Biggl|\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla p,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle\Biggr|\leq C\|\nabla p\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+C\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2},

(5.109)

\displaystyle I_{3}^{\prime}\leq\Biggl|\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}n,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u_{2}\rangle\Biggr|

\displaystyle\leq C\|n\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+C\|u_{2}\|_{Y^{1}}^{2},

(5.110)

\displaystyle I_{4}^{\prime}\leq\Biggl|\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}U,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\rangle\Biggr|

\displaystyle\leq C\|U\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+C\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}.

(5.111)

Next we estimate the pressure. Multiplying

\partial^{\tau}_{t}
to both sides of equation (
2.12
) and taking the inner product with
\partial^{\tau}_{t}p
, this yields

\displaystyle-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\Delta p,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla\cdot\left(u^{a}\cdot\nabla u+u\cdot\nabla u^{a}+u\cdot\nabla u\right),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{y}n,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p\rangle-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla\cdot U,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p\rangle.

For the left term, by integration by parts, it shows

\displaystyle-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\Delta p,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{x}p,\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{x}p\rangle+\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{y}p,\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{y}p\rangle-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{y}p\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla p\|_{2}^{2}-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{y}p\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}dx.

(5.112)

For the right one of
\nabla\cdot\left(u^{a}\cdot\nabla u+u\cdot\nabla u^{a}+u\cdot\nabla u\right)
, by integration by parts, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla\cdot\left(u^{a}\cdot\nabla u+u\cdot\nabla u^{a}+u\cdot\nabla u\right),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p\rangle

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\left(u^{a}\cdot\nabla u+u\cdot\nabla u^{a}+u\cdot\nabla u\right),\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla p\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla u_{2}+u\cdot\nabla u_{2}^{a}+u\cdot\nabla u_{2})\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx,

which is bounded by

\displaystyle\leq C\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u^{a}\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla u\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla p\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla u^{a}\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla p\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}u\|_{\infty}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla u\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}\Biggl(\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla p\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)^{\!\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla u_{2}+u\cdot\nabla u_{2}^{a}+u\cdot\nabla u_{2})\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}dx,

and by Lemma
5.2
and Lemma
5.4
, this becomes

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\mu}\left(\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}^{2}\right)\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C\mu\|\nabla p\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\frac{C}{\mu}\|u\|_{Y^{1,2}}\|\nabla u\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{3}+C\mu\|\nabla p\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla u_{2}+u\cdot\nabla u_{2}^{a}+u\cdot\nabla u_{2})\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\mu}\left(1+\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}^{2}\right)\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C}{\mu}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,3}}^{2}\right)^{3}+C\mu\|\nabla p\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}

(5.113)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla u_{2}+u\cdot\nabla u_{2}^{a}+u\cdot\nabla u_{2})\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx.

For
\partial_{y}n
, it follows that

\displaystyle-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{y}n,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}n,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{y}p\rangle-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}n\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\mu}\|n\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+C\mu\|\partial_{y}p\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}n\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx.

(5.114)

For
\nabla\cdot U
, by integration by parts, this yields

\displaystyle-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla\cdot U,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\langle\partial^{\tau}_{t}U,\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla p\rangle-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}U_{2}\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\mu}\|U\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+C\mu\|\nabla p\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}U_{2}\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx.

(5.115)

Finally, by (
5.3
), (
5.3
), (
5.3
) and (
5.3
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\|\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla p\|_{2}^{2}-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}\partial_{y}p\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{C}{\mu}\left(1+\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}^{2}\right)\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{C}{\mu}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,3}}^{2}\right)^{3}+C\mu\|\nabla p\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}\left(u^{a}\cdot\nabla u_{2}+u\cdot\nabla u_{2}^{a}+u\cdot\nabla u_{2}\;\right)\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{C}{\mu}\|n\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+C\mu\|\partial_{y}p\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}n\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{C}{\mu}\|U\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+C\mu\|\nabla p\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}U_{2}\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx.

For the second term in equation (
2.11
) involving
u
, we simplify it as follows:

\displaystyle(1-C\mu)\|\nabla p\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\mu}\left(1+\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}^{2}\right)

(5.116)

\displaystyle\times\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{2}+\|n\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\mu}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,3}}^{2}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}\left(\partial_{t}u_{2}-\varepsilon^{2}\Delta u_{2}\;\right)\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx+\frac{C}{\mu}\|U\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}.

By
\nabla\cdot u=0
and the boundary conditions (
\overline{\partial_{y}u_{1}}=0,\overline{\partial_{y}^{2}u_{2}}=-\overline{\partial_{xy}u_{1}}=0
), there holds:

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}\left(\partial_{t}u_{2}-\varepsilon^{2}\Delta u_{2}\;\right)\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial^{\tau}_{t}(-\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}f+\varepsilon^{4}\partial_{x}^{2}f)\;\overline{\partial^{\tau}_{t}p}\,dx

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\partial^{\tau}_{t}(-\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}f+\varepsilon^{4}\partial_{x}^{2}f)\;\partial_{y}\left(\partial^{\tau}_{t}p\;e^{-y}\right)\,dx\,dy,

and applying Hölder inequality, we know that

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\partial^{\tau}_{t}(-\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}f+\varepsilon^{4}\partial_{x}^{2}f)\;\partial_{y}\partial^{\tau}_{t}p\;e^{-y}\,dx\,dy+\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\partial^{\tau}_{t}(\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}f-\varepsilon^{4}\partial_{x}^{2}f)\;\partial^{\tau}_{t}p\;e^{-y}\,dx\,dy\right)

(5.117)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\tau\leq 1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\partial^{\tau}_{t}(\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}f-\varepsilon^{4}\partial_{x}^{2}f)\;\partial_{y}\partial^{\tau}_{t}p\;e^{-y}\,dx\,dy+\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\partial^{\tau}_{t}(\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}F-\varepsilon^{4}\partial_{x}f)\;\partial_{x}\partial^{\tau}_{t}p\;e^{-y}\,dx\,dy\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\left(\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{2,2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,3}}\right)\|\partial_{y}p\|_{Y^{1}}+C\varepsilon^{2}\left(\|Fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{2,2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,2}}\right)\|\partial_{x}p\|_{Y^{1}},

where the definition of
F
is in (
2.23
).
Combining (
5.116
) and (
5.117
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(1-C\mu)\|\nabla p\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1}}^{2}\right)\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,1}}^{2}+\|n\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,3}}^{2}\right)^{3}

(5.118)

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{4}\left(\|Fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{2,2}}^{2}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{2,2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,3}}^{2}\right)+C\|U\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}.

In summary, we can prove this proposition by (
5.3
) to (
5.111
) and (
5.3
).
MM

\square

Proof of Proposition
3.5
.

First, we make the boundary corrections on the
\omega
’s equation (
2.14
) by

\displaystyle\widehat{\omega}=\omega-\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{x}fe^{-y},

then we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\|_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Delta\widehat{\omega},\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla\widehat{\omega}+u\cdot\nabla\omega^{a}+u\cdot\nabla\widehat{\omega}),\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle\bigr|+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x}n,\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}\left(\partial_{t}+(u^{a}+u)\cdot\nabla-\varepsilon^{2}\Delta\right)(\partial_{x}fe^{-y}),\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle\bigr|+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}\nabla^{\perp}\cdot U,\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\quad=:\bar{C}_{1}+\bar{C}_{2}+\bar{C}_{3}+\bar{C}_{4}.

For the left, by integration by parts, we get

\displaystyle-\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Delta\widehat{\omega},\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x}\widehat{\omega},\;\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x}\widehat{\omega}\rangle+\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}\widehat{\omega},\;\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}\widehat{\omega}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}\widehat{\omega},\;[\partial_{y},\partial^{\alpha}]\widehat{\omega}\rangle-\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle[\partial^{\alpha},\partial_{y}]\partial_{y}\widehat{\omega},\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle,

which equals with

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\widehat{\omega}\|_{2}^{2}-C\delta\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}\widehat{\omega},\;\partial^{\alpha-(0,0,1)}\partial_{y}\widehat{\omega}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\widehat{\omega}\|_{2}^{2}-C\delta\varepsilon^{2}\Biggl(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{y}\widehat{\omega}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha-(0,0,1)}\partial_{y}\widehat{\omega}\|_{2}^{2}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\geq(1-C\delta)\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\widehat{\omega}\|_{2}^{2}.

(5.119)

Estimate of
\bar{C}_{1}
.
We have

\displaystyle\bar{C}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla\widehat{\omega}),\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle\bigr|+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u\cdot\nabla\omega^{a}),\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u\cdot\nabla\widehat{\omega}),\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle\bigr|.

For the first term
u^{a}\cdot\nabla\widehat{\omega}
, by Lemma
5.5
(a), it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u^{a}\cdot\nabla\widehat{\omega}),\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma}\left(1+\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m}}\right)^{2}\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{4}\|\partial_{y}\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

For the second term
u\cdot\nabla\omega^{a}
, by Lemma
5.5
(b), this yields

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u\cdot\nabla\omega^{a}),\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left(\|\omega^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+1\right)\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\left(\|\omega^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}\omega^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left(\|\omega^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+1\right)\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\left(1+\|\omega^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}\omega^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right).

For the last term
u\cdot\nabla\widehat{\omega}
, by Lemma
5.5
(c), we derive

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}(u\cdot\nabla\widehat{\omega}),\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sigma}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C}{\sigma\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C}{\sigma}\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sigma}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C}{\sigma\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C}{\sigma}\varepsilon^{2}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

Summing up, we get

\displaystyle\bar{C}_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\delta}}{\sigma}\left(1+\|u^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}+\|\omega^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}\right)^{2}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

(5.120)

\displaystyle+C\sigma\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\frac{C}{\sigma\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\sigma}\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+1}}^{2}\left(1+\|\omega^{a}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}\omega^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right).

Estimate of
\bar{C}_{2}
.
, by Hölder inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\bar{C}_{2}\leq C\sigma\|\nabla n\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\frac{C}{\sigma}\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

(5.121)

Estimate of
\bar{C}_{3}
.
We now estimate each term in
\bar{C}_{3}
separately. First,
\bar{C}_{3}
is decomposed into three parts:

\displaystyle\bar{C}_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{t}(\partial_{x}fe^{-y}),\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle\bigr|+\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}((u^{a}+u)\cdot\nabla)\partial_{x}fe^{-y},\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl|\langle\partial^{\alpha}\varepsilon^{2}\Delta(\partial_{x}fe^{-y}),\;\partial^{\alpha}\widehat{\omega}\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle=:\bar{C}_{31}+\bar{C}_{32}+\bar{C}_{33}.

Next for
\bar{C}_{31}
, the estimate is

\displaystyle\bar{C}_{31}\leq\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{2,m+2}}^{2}+\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

Then for
\bar{C}_{32}
, applying Hölder inequality and Lemma
5.4
gives

\displaystyle\bar{C}_{32}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\|u^{a}+u\|_{Y^{1,m}}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+2}}\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\left(\|u^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m-1}}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m}}\right)\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+2}}\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,m+2}}^{2}+C\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{Y^{1,m-1}}^{2}+\|u^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+3}}^{2}+C\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m-1}}^{2}+\|u^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

Finally for
\bar{C}_{33}
,

\displaystyle\bar{C}_{33}\leq C\varepsilon^{8}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+3}}^{2}+C\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

In summary, we get

\displaystyle\bar{C}_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{2,m+2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+3}}^{2}

(5.122)

\displaystyle+C\left(1+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m-1}}^{2}+\|u^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{2,m+2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{4}\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m+3}}^{2}
