\displaystyle+C\sigma\left(1+\|u^{a}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)\left(\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}\right)+\frac{C\sigma}{\varepsilon^{4}}\left(\|u\|_{Y^{1}}^{2}+\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}\right)^{3}.

Estimate of
\bar{C}_{4}
.
We have

\displaystyle C_{4}\leq C\|\nabla^{\perp}\cdot U\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}+C\|\widehat{\omega}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}.

(5.123)

In summary, this proposition can be proven by using (
5.3
)-(
5.123
).
MM

\square

6.
Appendix

This appendix is devoted to prove Proposition
2.1
. Similar to Section
2.2
, we define:

\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}=\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{1}}\partial_{x}^{\alpha_{2}}(\delta z)^{\alpha_{3}}\partial_{z}^{\alpha_{3}}.

(6.124)

Then we have

[\partial_{z},\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}]=\delta\alpha_{3}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha-(0,0,1)}\partial_{z},

(6.125)

and for
s,l,m\in\mathbb{N}
, the inner conormal Sobolev space is defined by

Z^{l,m}_{s}\left(\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}\right)=\left\{u\;\Bigg|\;\|u\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}^{2}=\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\|(1+z)^{s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}u\|_{2}^{2}<\infty\right\}.

(6.126)

Then by the definitions (
2.18
) and (
2.19
) we have

\displaystyle\|h(t,x,y)\|_{Y^{l,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\int_{R_{+}^{2}}\left|\partial_{t}^{l}\partial_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}\psi^{\alpha_{3}}\partial_{y}^{\alpha_{3}}h\right|^{2}dxdy

(6.127)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\left\|\psi^{\alpha_{3}}\right\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{R_{+}^{2}}\left|\partial_{t}^{l}\partial_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}\partial_{y}^{\alpha_{3}}h\right|^{2}dxdy

\displaystyle\leq C\|h\|_{\overline{H}^{l,m}}^{2},

and

\displaystyle\|h(t,x,z)\|_{Y^{l,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\int_{R_{+}^{2}}\left|\partial_{t}^{l}\partial_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}\frac{\psi^{\alpha_{3}}}{(\delta z)^{\alpha_{3}}}\frac{(\delta z)^{\alpha_{3}}}{\varepsilon^{\alpha_{3}}}\partial_{z}^{\alpha_{3}}h\right|^{2}dxdy

(6.128)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\left\|\frac{\psi^{\alpha_{3}}}{(\delta y)^{\alpha_{3}}}\right\|_{L_{y}^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{R_{+}^{2}}\left|\partial_{t}^{l}\partial_{x}^{\alpha_{1}}(\delta z)^{\alpha_{3}}\partial_{z}^{\alpha_{3}}h\right|^{2}dxd(\varepsilon z)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon\|h\|_{Z_{0}^{l,m}}^{2},

which indicates that we need to compute the Sobolev norm of the outer solution and the conormal Sobolev norm of the inner solution, respectively.

In this section, we denote

\displaystyle\|(n,c,u)\|_{Y^{l,m}}^{2}=\|n\|_{Y^{l,m}}^{2}+\|c\|_{Y^{l,m}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Y^{l,m}}^{2},

(6.129)

\displaystyle\|(n,c,u)\|_{Y^{l,m}_{\infty}}^{2}=\|n\|_{Y^{l,m}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|c\|_{Y^{l,m}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Y^{l,m}_{\infty}}^{2},

\displaystyle\|(n,c,u)\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}^{2}=\|n\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}^{2}+\|c\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}^{2}+\|u\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}^{2}.

6.1.
Local well-posedness of solutions to the equations (
4.51
) in
\overline{H}^{l,m}
.

Recall the space
\overline{H}^{l,m}
defined in (
2.19
).
Assume that the
m
-th order compatibility conditions hold:

(\mathcal{I}^{m})\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial^{\tau+1}_{t}n^{e,0}(x,y,0)+\partial^{\tau}_{t}({u}^{0}\cdot\nabla n^{e,0})(x,y,0)-\partial^{\tau}_{t}\Delta n^{e,0}(x,y,0)=-\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla\cdot(n^{e,0}\nabla c^{e,0})(x,y,0),\\
&\partial^{\tau+1}_{t}c^{e,0}(x,y,0)+\partial^{\tau}_{t}({u^{e,0}}\cdot\nabla c^{e,0})(x,y,0)=-\partial^{\tau}_{t}(c^{e,0}n^{e,0})(x,y,0),\\
&\partial^{\tau+1}_{t}{u^{e,0}}(x,y,0)+\partial^{\tau}_{t}({u^{e,0}}\cdot\nabla{u^{e,0}})(x,y,0)+\partial^{\tau}_{t}\nabla p^{e,0}(x,y,0)=-\binom{0}{\partial^{\tau}_{t}n^{e,0}(x,y,0)},\\
&0\leq 2\tau\leq m.\end{aligned}\right.

Then the local well-posed results of the system (
4.51
) are stated as follows:

Proposition 6.1
.

Assume that
(n_{in},c_{in},u_{in})\in\left(H^{m+1}\times H^{m+1}\times H^{m+1}\right)(3\leq m\in\mathbb{N})
fulfills the compatibility conditions
(\mathcal{I}^{m-1})
. Then (
4.51
) admits a unique solution
(n^{e,0},c^{e,0},u^{e,0},\nabla p^{e,0})
, whose maximal time of existence is denoted by
0<T_{e}\leq\infty
, satisfying for any
0<T<T_{e}

\displaystyle(n^{0},c^{0},u^{0})\in C\left([0,T];H^{m}\times H^{m+1}\times H^{m+1}\right)\cap L^{2}\left(0,T;H^{m+1}\times H^{m+1}\times H^{m+1}\right),

\displaystyle\nabla p^{e,0}\in L^{\infty}\left(0,T;H^{m}\right),

and

\displaystyle n^{e,0}

\displaystyle\in C\left([0,T];\overline{H}^{k,m-2k}\right)\cap L^{2}\left(0,T;\overline{H}^{k,m+1-2k}\right),\quad 2k\leq m,

\displaystyle c^{e,0},u^{e,0}

\displaystyle\in C\left([0,T];\overline{H}^{k,m+1-2k}\right)\cap L^{2}\left(0,T;\overline{H}^{k,m+1-2k}\right),\quad 2<2k\leq m,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}c^{e,0},\partial_{t}u^{e,0}

\displaystyle\in C\left([0,T];H^{m}\right)\cap L^{2}\left(0,T;H^{m}\right).

Before proving Proposition
6.1
, we give the following Korn’s type lemma and Kato-Ponce commutator estimates, which are frequently used in the proof.

Lemma 6.2

(
[
36
]
)
.

Let
u=(u_{1},u_{2})
be a vector field on the upper half-plane
\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}=\{(x,y)\in\mathbb{R}^{2}:y>0\}
, satisfying the vanishing property at
\infty
,

\mathrm{div}\,u=0

with the boundary condition
u_{2}|_{y=0}=0
. Denote
\omega=\nabla\times\,u=\partial_{x}u_{2}-\partial_{y}u_{1}
, then for any integer
k\geq 0
, there exists a constant
C=C(k)>0
depending only on
k
, such that

\begin{split}{}\left\|\nabla^{k+1}u\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2})}\leq C\left\|\nabla^{k}\omega\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2})}.\end{split}

Lemma 6.3

(
[
12
,
42
]
)
.

For
\alpha,\beta\in\mathbb{N}^{3}
, there hold

\displaystyle\left\|[D^{\alpha},f]D^{\beta}g\right\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\left(\sum_{|\gamma|=|\alpha|+|\beta|}\|D^{\gamma}f\|_{L^{2}}\|g\|_{L^{\infty}}+\sum_{|\gamma|=|\alpha|+|\beta|-1}\|\nabla f\|_{L^{\infty}}\|D^{\gamma}g\|_{L^{2}}\right),

and

\displaystyle\left\|D^{\alpha}(fg)\right\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\left(\sum_{|\gamma|=|\alpha|}\|D^{\gamma}f\|_{L^{2}}\|g\|_{L^{\infty}}+\sum_{|\gamma|=|\alpha|}\|f\|_{L^{\infty}}\|D^{\gamma}g\|_{L^{2}}\right).

Proof of Proposition
6.1
.

To overcome the loss of derivatives caused by the chemotaxis term
\nabla\cdot(n^{e,0}\nabla c^{e,0})
and the boundary commutator issues in the half-plane, we assume
m\geq 3
(which ensures
H^{m}(\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2})\hookrightarrow W^{1,\infty}(\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2})
) and define the high-order total energy functional:

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{m}(t)=

\displaystyle\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\omega^{e,0}(t)\|_{H^{m}}^{2}+\|c^{e,0}(t)\|_{H^{m+1}}^{2}

(6.130)

\displaystyle+|||\nabla n^{e,0}(t)|||_{m-1}+|||\partial_{t}n^{e,0}(t)|||_{m-1}+\|n^{e,0}\|_{2},

where
|||n^{e,0}|||_{m}
is the anisotropic energy norm involving only time and tangential spatial derivatives:

|||n^{e,0}(t)|||_{m}^{2}=\sum_{2\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq m}\|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{1}}\partial_{x}^{\alpha_{2}}n^{e,0}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2})}^{2}.

(6.131)

We will establish a closed
a priori
estimate for
\mathcal{E}_{m}(t)
.

Step 1: Estimate for the velocity
u^{e,0}
.

To avoid estimating the pressure
p^{0}
, we take the curl of the Euler equation. Let
\omega^{e,0}=\nabla^{\perp}\cdot u^{e,0}=\partial_{x}u_{2}^{0}-\partial_{y}u_{1}^{0}
, which satisfies:

\partial_{t}\omega^{e,0}+u^{e,0}\cdot\nabla\omega^{e,0}=\partial_{x}n^{e,0}.

(6.132)

Applying
\nabla^{l}
to the vorticity equation and taking the
L^{2}
inner product with
\nabla^{l}\omega^{e,0}
(
0\leq l\leq m
), the boundary integral of the convective term vanishes since
u_{2}^{0}|_{y=0}=0
,
where
\sigma>0
is a small constant to be determined later.
Using Lemma
6.3
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\omega^{e,0}\|_{H^{m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla u^{e,0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\omega^{e,0}\|_{H^{m}}^{2}+C\|u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m}}\|\nabla\omega^{e,0}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}n^{e,0}\|_{H^{m}}\|\omega^{e,0}\|_{H^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}\|\omega^{e,0}\|_{H^{m}}^{2}+\sigma\|n^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}^{2}+C_{\sigma}\|\omega^{e,0}\|_{H^{m}}^{2}.

By Lemma
6.2
we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\omega^{e,0}\|_{H^{m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma\|n^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}^{2}+C_{\sigma}P(\mathcal{E}_{m}(t)),

(6.133)

where
P(\cdot)
denotes a generic polynomial and
\sigma>0
is a small constant to be determined later.

For derivatives with respect to
t
only, we apply
\partial_{t}^{k}
to the velocity equation and taking the
L^{2}
inner product with
\partial^{k}_{t}u^{e,0}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial^{k}_{t}u^{e,0}\|_{2}^{2}

(6.134)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{2k\leq m+1}|\langle\partial^{k}_{t}(u^{e,0}\cdot\nabla u^{e,0}),\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}\rangle|+\sum_{2k\leq m+1}|\langle\partial_{t}^{k}n,\partial^{k}_{t}u_{2}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}}^{2}\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial^{k}_{t}u^{e,0}\|_{2}+(|||\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}+\|n^{e,0}\|_{2})\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}_{2}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\mathcal{E}_{m}(t),\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}}^{2}\right).

Step 2:
H^{m+1}
estimates for the chemical concentration
c^{e,0}
.

Applying
\nabla^{l}
to the transport equation of
c^{e,0}
and taking the
L^{2}
inner product with
\nabla^{l}c^{e,0}(0\leq l\leq m+1)
, we get:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla u^{e,0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}^{2}+C\|\nabla c^{e,0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}\|c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}

(6.135)

\displaystyle+C\|c^{e,0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|n^{e,0}\|_{H^{m}}+\|c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}\|n^{e,0}\|_{L^{\infty}},

which implies that

\frac{d}{dt}\|c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}^{2}\leq\sigma\|n^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}^{2}+C_{\sigma}P(\mathcal{E}_{m}(t)).

(6.136)

Step 3: Anisotropic Estimate for
n^{e,0}
and Perfect Boundary Cancellation.

In the half-plane, normal derivatives do not commute with the boundary trace. Therefore, we define the tangential-time derivative operator
\partial_{\Gamma}^{\alpha}=\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{1}}\partial_{x}^{\alpha_{2}}
.
We rewrite the equation for
n^{e,0}
in the divergence form of the total flux:

\partial_{t}n^{e,0}+u^{e,0}\cdot\nabla n^{e,0}-\nabla\cdot(\nabla n^{e,0}-n^{e,0}\nabla c^{e,0})=0.

(6.137)

Applying
\partial_{t}\partial_{\Gamma}^{\alpha}
to the equation, multiplying by
\partial_{t}\partial_{\Gamma}^{\alpha}n^{e,0}
, integrating over
\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}
, and summing by
2\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq m-1
, we integrate the total flux term by parts (noting the outward normal vector is
-\vec{e}_{2}
):

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\nabla\cdot\partial_{t}\partial_{\Gamma}^{\alpha}(\nabla n^{e,0}-n^{e,0}\nabla c^{e,0})\partial_{t}\partial_{\Gamma}^{\alpha}n^{e,0}\,dxdy

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\partial_{t}\partial_{\Gamma}^{\alpha}(\nabla n^{e,0}-n^{e,0}\nabla c^{e,0})\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}\partial_{\Gamma}^{\alpha}n^{e,0}\,dxdy+\int_{y=0}\partial_{t}\partial_{\Gamma}^{\alpha}\underbrace{\left(\partial_{y}n^{e,0}-n^{e,0}\partial_{y}c^{e,0}\right)}_{\text{Total normal flux}}\partial_{t}\partial_{\Gamma}^{\alpha}n^{e,0}\,dx.

(6.138)

Note that the physical boundary condition
\partial_{y}n^{e,0}=n^{e,0}\partial_{y}c^{e,0}
, which implies the boundary integral of (
6.1
) identically vanishes.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}|||\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}+|||\nabla\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m-1}\left|\left\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}\left(\partial_{t}u^{e,0}\cdot\nabla n^{e,0}\right),\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}n^{e,0}\right\rangle\right|+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m-1}\left|\left\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}\left(u^{e,0}\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}n^{e,0}\right),\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}n^{e,0}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m-1}\left|\left\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}\left(\partial_{t}n^{e,0}\nabla c^{e,0}\right),\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}\nabla n^{e,0}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m-1}\left|\left\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}\left[n^{e,0}\left(\nabla u^{e,0}\cdot\nabla c^{e,0}+(u^{e,0}\cdot\nabla)\nabla c^{e,0}+\nabla c^{e,0}n^{e,0}+c^{e,0}\nabla n^{e,0}\right)\right],\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}\nabla n^{e,0}\right\rangle\right|.

By the divergence-free condition, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}|||\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}+|||\nabla\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}}\left(\sum_{l\leq m}|||\partial_{y}^{l}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l}+|||\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}\right)|||\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}

\displaystyle+C\left(|||\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}+\sum_{l\leq m}|||\partial_{y}^{l}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l}\right)\sum_{2k\leq m}\left\|\partial^{k}_{t}c^{e,0}\right\|_{H^{m-2k}}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}\nabla n^{e,0}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\sum_{l\leq m-1}|||\partial_{y}^{l}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1-l}\sum_{2k\leq m}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m-2k}}\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial_{t}^{k}c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}\nabla n^{e,0}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\sum_{l\leq m}|||\partial_{y}^{l}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l}^{2}\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial_{t}^{k}c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}\nabla n^{e,0}\right\|_{2}

which implies

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}|||\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}+|||\nabla\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}

(6.139)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CP\big(\mathcal{E}_{m}(t),\sum_{l\leq m}|||\partial_{y}^{l}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l},\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial_{t}^{k}c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}},\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}}\big)

In addition, applying
\partial_{\Gamma}^{\alpha}
to the equation, multiplying by
\partial_{t}\partial_{\Gamma}^{\alpha}n^{e,0}
, then by a similar process, we can get:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}|||\nabla n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}+|||\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}

(6.140)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m-1}\left|\left\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}(u^{e,0}\cdot\nabla n^{e,0}),\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}n^{e,0}\right\rangle\right|+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m-1}\left|\left\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}(n^{e,0}\nabla c^{e,0}),\partial^{\alpha}_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}\nabla n^{e,0}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{2k\leq m-1}||\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}||_{H^{m-1-2k}}\sum_{l\leq m}|||\partial^{l}_{y}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l}|||\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{l\leq m-1}|||\partial_{y}^{l}n^{e,0}|||_{{m-1-l}}\sum_{2k\leq m}||\partial_{t}^{k}c^{e,0}||_{H^{m-2k}}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq m-1}|||\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{t}\nabla n^{e,0}|||_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sigma|||\partial_{t}\nabla n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|||\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{\sigma}P\big(\sum_{l\leq m}|||\partial_{y}^{l}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l},\sum_{2k\leq m}\|\partial_{t}^{k}c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m-2k}},\sum_{2k\leq m}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m-2k}}\big).

Finally, multiplying
n^{e,0}
on the both sides of equation, we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}||n^{e,0}||_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}||\nabla n^{e,0}||_{2}^{2}\leq CP\big(\mathcal{E}_{m}(t)\big).

(6.141)

Combining (
6.139
), (
6.140
) and (
6.141
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(|||\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}+|||\nabla n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}+||n^{e,0}||_{2}^{2}\right)+|||\nabla\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}

(6.142)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CP\left(\mathcal{E}_{m}(t),\sum_{l\leq m}|||\partial_{y}^{l}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l},\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial_{t}^{k}c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}},\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}}\right).

Step 4: Elliptic lifting for normal derivatives.

To recover the full isotropic norm
\|n^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}
, we utilize the equation itself as an elliptic equation in the normal direction
y
:

\partial_{y}^{2}n^{e,0}=\partial_{t}n^{e,0}+u^{e,0}\cdot\nabla n^{e,0}-\partial_{x}^{2}n^{e,0}+\nabla\cdot(n^{e,0}\nabla c^{e,0}).

(6.143)

To control
\partial_{y}^{k}n^{e,0}
, we only need to control terms on the right-hand side which involve at most
\partial_{y}^{k-1}n^{e,0}
, tangential derivatives
\partial_{x}
, and the time derivative
\partial_{t}
. The inclusion of time derivatives in our anisotropic norm
|||n^{e,0}|||_{m}
ensures that terms like
\partial_{t}n^{e,0}\in H^{m-2}
are well-controlled.

To be precise,
let
\mathcal{M}(l)
denote the claim:

\displaystyle|||\partial_{y}^{l}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l+1}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle P\left(\mathcal{E}_{m}(t),\sum_{2k\leq m}\|\partial_{t}^{k}c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}},\sum_{2k\leq m}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}}\right)+|||\nabla n^{e,0}|||_{m}^{2},

(6.144)

which is related to
l
. Then by noting that

\displaystyle\|n^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}^{2}\leq\sum_{l\leq m+1}|||\partial_{y}^{l}n^{e,0}|||_{m+1-l},

(6.145)

it suffices to show that
\mathcal{M}(l)
holds for all
0\leq l\leq m+1
for the estimate of
\|n^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}^{2}
in (
6.136
).
We prove (
6.144
) by mathematical induction.

(
I
Basis Step.) For
l=0
and
l=1
, we have

\displaystyle|||n^{e,0}|||_{m+1}+|||\partial_{y}n^{e,0}|||_{m}\leq\|n^{e,0}\|_{2}+|||\nabla n^{e,0}|||_{m}+|||\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}.

(
II
Inductive Step.) Assume
\mathcal{M}(l)
holds for all
1\leq l\leq l_{0}\leq m
, then for
l=l_{0}+1
, by (
6.143
) we have

\displaystyle|||\partial_{y}^{l_{0}+1}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l_{0}}=

\displaystyle|||\partial_{y}^{l_{0}-1}\partial_{y}^{2}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l_{0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|||\partial_{y}^{l_{0}-1}\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l_{0}}+|||\partial_{y}^{l_{0}-1}\partial_{x}^{2}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l_{0}}

\displaystyle+|||\partial_{y}^{l_{0}-1}(u^{e,0}\cdot\nabla n^{e,0})|||_{m-l_{0}}+|||\partial_{y}^{l_{0}-1}\nabla\cdot(n\nabla c)|||_{m-l_{0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|||\partial_{y}^{l_{0}-1}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l_{0}+2}+\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}}\sum_{l\leq l_{0}}|||\partial_{y}^{l}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l}

\displaystyle+\sum_{2k\leq m+1}\|\partial_{t}^{k}c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}}\sum_{l\leq l_{0}}|||\partial_{y}^{l}n^{e,0}|||_{m-l},

Thus, by the inductive hypothesis, the inductive step is proved.

Moreover, for
c
and
u
, we denote the claim
\mathcal{N}(k)
by

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k}c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}}+\|\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1-2k}}\leq P(\mathcal{E}_{m}(t)),

(6.146)

which is related to
k
. It is suffices to show that
\mathcal{N}(k)
holds for all
0\leq 2k\leq m+1
, and we use mathematical induction again.

(
I
Basis Step.) For
k=0
, by Lemma
6.2
we have

\displaystyle\|c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}+\|u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}\leq\mathcal{E}_{m}(t).

(
II
Inductive Step.) Assume
\mathcal{N}(K)
holds for all
k\leq k_{0}\leq\lfloor\frac{m+1}{2}\rfloor-1
, then for
k=k_{0}+1
, by equations (
4.51
)
2
and (
4.51
)
3
we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k_{0}+1}c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m-2(k_{0}+1)}}=

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k_{0}}\partial_{t}c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m-2(k_{0}+1)}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k_{0}}(u^{e,0}\cdot\nabla c^{e,0})\|_{H^{m-2(k_{0}+1)}}+\|\partial_{t}^{k_{0}}(c^{e,0}n^{e,0})\|_{H^{m-2(k_{0}+1)}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k\leq k_{0}}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m-2k}}\sum_{k\leq k_{0}}\|\partial_{t}^{k}c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m-2k-1}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k\leq k_{0}}\|\partial_{t}^{k_{0}}c^{e,0}\|_{H^{m-2k-1}}\sum_{l\leq m-1}|||\partial_{y}^{l}n^{e,0}|||_{H^{m-l-1}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k_{0}+1}u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m-2(k_{0}+1)}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k_{0}+1}u^{e,0}\|_{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{k_{0}+1}\nabla u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m-2k_{0}-3}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k_{0}+1}u^{e,0}\|_{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{k_{0}}\partial_{t}\omega^{e,0}\|_{H^{m-2k_{0}-3}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k_{0}+1}u^{e,0}\|_{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{k_{0}}(u^{e,0}\cdot\nabla\omega^{e,0})\|_{H^{m-2k_{0}-3}}+\|\partial_{t}^{k_{0}}\partial_{x}n^{e,0}\|_{H^{m-2k_{0}-3}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k_{0}+1}u^{e,0}\|_{2}+\sum_{k\leq k_{0}}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u^{e,0}\|_{H^{m-2k-1}}\sum_{k\leq k_{0}}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\omega^{e,0}\|_{H^{m-2k-2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{l\leq m-2}|||\partial_{y}^{l}n^{e,0}|||_{H^{m-l-2}}.

Thus, by the inductive hypothesis and (
6.144
), the inductive step is proved.

Combining (
6.139
) to (
6.141
), (
6.144
) and (
6.146
), there exists a dissipation

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(|||\nabla n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}+|||\partial_{t}n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}+\|n\|_{2}^{2}\right)+|||\partial_{t}\nabla n^{e,0}|||_{m-1}^{2}\leq P(\mathcal{E}_{m}(t)).

(6.147)

Step 5: Energy closure and conclusion.

Summing the estimates (
6.133
), (
6.134
), (
6.136
), and (
6.147
), we have:

\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}_{m}(t)+C(1-\sigma)\|n^{e,0}\|_{H^{m+1}}^{2}\leq CP(\mathcal{E}_{m}(t)).

(6.148)

Since the initial data
(u_{in},c_{in},n_{in})\in H^{m+1}\times H^{m+1}\times H^{m}
, we have
\mathcal{E}_{m}(0)<\infty
. By the standard ODE comparison principle, there exists a local time
T>0
such that
\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\mathcal{E}_{m}(t)\leq 2\mathcal{E}_{m}(0)<\infty
.
The existence of the solution follows from constructing a successive approximation scheme (e.g., Friedrichs mollifiers) and passing to the limit using the uniform bounds derived above and the Aubin-Lions compactness lemma. The uniqueness follows from standard
L^{2}
stability estimates for the difference of two solutions
(\delta u,\delta c,\delta n)
, utilizing the Lipschitz continuity of the flow (guaranteed by
m\geq 3
).

Thus, the system is locally well-posed in the specified Sobolev spaces.

MM

\square

6.2.
Well-posedness of boundary layers.

The purpose of this section is to obtain some necessary a priori estimates of
n^{b,1},c^{b,1},(u_{1}^{b,1})_{p}
and
n^{b,2},c^{b,2},u_{1}^{b,2}
. The main results are as follows:

Proposition 6.4
.

Let
m\geq 2,s\geq 2\in\mathbb{N}
. Assume that
(n^{e,0},c^{e,0},u^{e,0})\in\overline{H}^{3,m+3}
defined in (
2.19
) are the same as in Proposition
6.1
, then there exists
T^{b,1}\geq 0
, such that for all
t\in[0,T^{b,1}]
and
\delta
small enough, the systems (
4.65
) and (
4.66
) admit a unique solution
(n^{b,1},c^{b,1},(u^{b,1})_{p})
, which satisfy

\displaystyle\|(c^{b,1},u_{1}^{b,1},\partial_{z}c^{b,1},\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1})\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}Z^{2,{m}}_{s}}^{2}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1},\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1})\|_{L_{t}^{2}Z^{2,{m}}_{s}}^{2}\leq C,

(6.149)

and

\displaystyle\|(n^{b,1},\partial_{z}n^{b,1})\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}n^{b,1}\|_{L_{t}^{2}Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}+\|(u_{2}^{b,2},\partial_{z}u_{2}^{b,2},\partial_{z}^{2}u_{2}^{b,2})\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}Z_{s-2}^{2,m-1}}^{2}\leq C,~s\geq 2,

(6.150)

where
Z_{s}^{2,m}
is defined in (
6.126
).

Proposition 6.5
.

Let
m,s\in\mathbb{N}
. Assume that
(n^{e,0},c^{e,0},u^{e,0})\in\overline{H}^{3,m+4}
are the same as in Proposition
6.1
and
n^{b,1},c^{b,1},u_{1}^{b,1}\in Z^{2,m+1}_{s+3}
are the same as in Propositon
6.4
, then there exists
T^{b,2}\geq 0
, such that for all
t\in[0,T^{b,2}]
and
\delta
small enough, the systems (
4.69
)
and (
4.70
) admit a unique solution
(n^{b,2},c^{b,2},u^{b,2}_{1})
, which satisfy

\displaystyle\|(c^{b,2},u_{1}^{b,2},\partial_{z}c^{b,2},\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,2})\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}Z^{2,{m}}_{s}}^{2}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,2},\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,2})\|_{L_{t}^{2}Z^{2,{m}}_{s}}^{2}\leq C,

and

\displaystyle\|n^{b,2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}Z^{2,{m}}_{s}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}n^{b,2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}Z^{2,{m}}_{s}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}n^{b,2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}Z^{2,{m}}_{s}}^{2}\leq C.

Before proving Proposition
6.4
and Proposition
6.5
, we give the following lemmas, which are frequently used in
the proof.

Lemma 6.6
.

Let

A=\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\Bigl|\Bigl\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\left((u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}G\right),(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\Bigr\rangle\Bigr|.

Then there holds

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle\leq C_{\delta}\Biggl(\left\|u_{1}^{e,0}\right\|_{\overline{H}^{l,m+2}}+\left\|\partial_{y}u_{2}^{e,0}\right\|_{\overline{H}^{l,m+2}}\Biggr)\|G\bigr\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}^{2}.

(6.151)

Lemma 6.7
.

Let

B=\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\Bigl|\Bigl\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}(HG),(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}Q\Bigr\rangle\Bigr|.

Then the following hold:

(a) when
H=H(t,x)
we have

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,\alpha_{3}=0,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}H\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\|G\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}\|Q\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}},

(6.152)

(b) when
H=H(t,x,z)
we have

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle\leq C\|H\|_{Z_{0}^{l,m+1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{z}H\|_{Z_{0}^{l,m+1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|G\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}\|Q\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}.

(6.153)

Lemma 6.8
.

Letting

D=-\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\Bigl\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}^{2}H,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}H\Bigr\rangle,

and
H\partial_{z}H|_{z=0}=0
, we have

\displaystyle D\geq(1-C\sigma-C\delta)\|\partial_{z}H\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}^{2}-\frac{C}{\sigma}\|H\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s-1}},

(6.154)

where
\delta,\sigma>0.

Proof of Proposition
6.4
.

We briefly sketch the proof.

Step I. The estimates of
c^{b,1}
and
u_{1}^{b,1}
.
For
c^{b,1}
, applying
\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}
to the equation (
4.66
), multiplying by
(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}c^{b,1}
, integrating over
\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}
, and summing by
|\alpha|\leq m
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 2,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\partial_{t}c^{b,1},(1+z)^{2s}\,\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}c^{b,1}\bigr\rangle-\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 2,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1},(1+z)^{2s}\,\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}c^{b,1}\bigr\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\Bigl|\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 2,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\bigl(u_{1}^{b,1}\partial_{x}c^{E,0}\bigr),(1+z)^{2s}\,\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}c^{b,1}\bigr\rangle\Bigr|+\Bigl|\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 2,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\bigl((u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}c^{b,1}\bigr),(1+z)^{2s}\,\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}c^{b,1}\bigr\rangle\Bigr|

\displaystyle\quad+\Bigl|\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 2,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\bigl(c^{b,1}n^{E,0}+c^{E,0}n^{b,1}\bigr),(1+z)^{2s}\,\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}c^{b,1}\bigr\rangle\Bigr|.

For
u_{1}^{b,1}
, applying
\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}
to the equation (
4.65
)
1
, multiplying by
(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}u_{1}^{b,1}
, integrating over
\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}
, and summing by
|\alpha|\leq m
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 2,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\partial_{t}u_{1}^{b,1},(1+z)^{2s}\,\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}u_{1}^{b,1}\bigr\rangle-\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 2,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1},(1+z)^{2s}\,\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}u_{1}^{b,1}\bigr\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Bigl|\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 2,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\bigl((u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}u_{1}^{b,1}\bigr),(1+z)^{2s}\,\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}u_{1}^{b,1}\bigr\rangle\Bigr|+\Bigl|\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 2,|\alpha|\leq m}\bigl\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\bigl(u_{1}^{b,1}\partial_{x}u_{1}^{E,0}\bigr),(1+z)^{2s}\,\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}u_{1}^{b,1}\bigr\rangle\Bigr|.

Using (
6.151
), one could estimate the terms of
(u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}c^{b,1}
and
(u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}u_{1}^{b,1}
. The bound of the terms
u_{1}^{b,1}\partial_{x}c^{E,0}
,
u_{1}^{b,1}\partial_{x}u_{1}^{E,0}
and
c^{b,1}n^{E,0},c^{E,0}n^{b,1}
follows from (
6.152
). Moreover, by (
6.154
), we get the estimates of
\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1},\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1}
. In summary, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}+(1-C\delta)\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}+(1-C\delta)\|\partial_{z}c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}

(6.155)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\delta}\left(1+\|n^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+2}}^{2}+\|c^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+3}}^{2}+\|u^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+3}}^{2}\right)\|c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\left(1+\|u^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+3}}\right)\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}+\|c^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+2}}^{2}+\|u^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+2}}^{2}.

Step II. The estimates of
\partial_{z}c^{b,1}
and
\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}
.
For
\partial_{z}c^{b,1},\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}
, firstly applying boundary corrections
\widehat{\partial_{z}c^{b,1}}=\partial_{z}c^{b,1}+e^{-z}\overline{\partial_{y}c^{e,0}}
and
\widehat{\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}}=\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}+e^{-z}\overline{\partial_{y}u_{1}^{e,0}}
to the equations (
4.66
) and (
4.65
)
1
, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\widehat{\partial_{z}c^{b,1}}-\partial_{z}^{2}\bigl(\widehat{\partial_{z}c^{b,1}}\bigr)+(u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}\bigl(\widehat{\partial_{z}c^{b,1}}\bigr)+\overline{\partial_{y}u_{2}^{e,0}}\bigl(\widehat{\partial_{z}c^{b,1}}\bigr)+\widehat{\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}}\overline{\partial_{x}c^{e,0}}+\bigl(\overline{c^{e,0}}+1\bigr)\overline{n^{e,0}}\bigl(\widehat{\partial_{z}c^{b,1}}\bigr)

\displaystyle=\left[\partial_{t}-\partial_{z}^{2}+(u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\nabla+\overline{\partial_{y}u_{2}^{e,0}}+\bigl(\overline{c^{e,0}}+1\bigr)\overline{n^{e,0}}\right]\bigl(e^{-z}\overline{\partial_{y}c^{e,0}}\bigr)+e^{-z}\overline{\partial_{y}u_{1}^{e,0}}\overline{\partial_{x}c^{e,0}},

and

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\widehat{\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}}-\partial_{z}^{2}\bigl(\widehat{\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}}\bigr)+(u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}\bigl(\widehat{\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}}\bigr)+\overline{\partial_{y}u_{2}^{e,0}}\bigl(\widehat{\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}}\bigr)+\bigl(\widehat{\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}}\bigr)\overline{\partial_{x}u_{1}^{e,0}}

\displaystyle=\left[\partial_{t}-\partial_{z}^{2}+(u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}+\overline{\partial_{y}u_{2}^{e,0}}+\overline{\partial_{x}u_{1}^{e,0}}\right]\bigl(e^{-z}\overline{\partial_{y}u_{1}^{e,0}}\bigr).

Applying
\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}
to the above equation , multiplying by
(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\widehat{\partial_{z}c^{b,1}},(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\widehat{\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}}
respectively, then by Lemma
6.6
to Lemma
6.8
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\widehat{\partial_{z}c^{b,1}}\right\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\widehat{\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}}\right\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}+(1-C\delta)\left\|\widehat{\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1}}\right\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}+(1-C\delta)\left\|\widehat{\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1}}\right\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}

(6.156)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\delta}\left(1+\|n^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+2}}^{2}+\|c^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+3}}^{2}+\|u^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+3}}^{2}\right)\left\|\widehat{\partial_{z}c^{b,1}}\right\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{\delta}\left(1+\|u^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+3}}+\|c^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+3}}^{2}\right)\left\|\widehat{\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}}\right\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\left(1+\|n^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+2}}^{2}+\|c^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{3,m+3}}^{2}+\|u^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{3,m+3}}^{2}\right)^{2}.

By noting that

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\|\partial_{z}c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t}\left\|\widehat{\partial_{z}c^{b,1}}\right\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}+\sup_{t}\|c^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+2}}^{2},

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t}\left\|\widehat{\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}}\right\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}+\sup_{t}\|u_{1}^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+2}}^{2},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1}\|_{L^{2}_{t}Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\widehat{\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}+\|c^{e,0}\|_{L^{2}_{t}\overline{H}^{2,m+3}}^{2},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{L^{2}_{t}Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\widehat{\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}+\|u_{1}^{e,0}\|_{L^{2}_{t}\overline{H}^{2,m+3}}^{2},

the inequality (
6.149
) is proved.

Step III. The estimates of
n^{b,1}
and
u_{2}^{b,2}
.
For
n^{b,1}
and
u_{2}^{b,2}
, by (
4.66
)
2
and (
4.65
)
2
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{2}\|\partial_{z}^{i}n^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}^{2}\leq C\|n^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{2,m+2}}\|\partial_{z}^{i}c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}},

and

\displaystyle\|u_{2}^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s-2}^{2,m-1}}=

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{z}^{\infty}\partial_{x}u_{1}^{b,1}d\zeta\right\|_{Z_{s-2}^{2,m-1}}\leq C\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{i}u_{2}^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s-2}^{2,m-1}}=

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{z}^{i-1}\partial_{x}u_{1}^{b,1}\right\|_{Z_{s-2}^{2,m-1}}\leq C\|\partial_{z}^{i-1}u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{2,m}}，

which
i=1,2
.
The proof is complete.

MM

\square

Proof of Proposition
6.5
.

The proof of Proposition
6.5
is similar to that of Proposition
6.4
, and we only present the energy inequality.

(a) For
n^{b,2}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\|n^{b,2}\|_{Z^{1,{m}}_{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|n^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+2}}\|c^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}+C\|c^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}\|n^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s+2}^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\left(\|n^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}+\|n^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}n^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)\|\partial_{z}c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s+3}^{1,m}}^{2}.

(b) For
\partial_{z}n^{b,2}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}n^{b,2}\|_{Z^{1,{m}}_{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|n^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+2}}\|\partial_{z}c^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}+C\|c^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}\|n^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\left(\|n^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}+\|n^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}n^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)\|\partial_{z}c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s+1}^{1,m}}^{2}.

(c) For
\partial_{z}^{2}n^{b,2}
, it shows that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}^{2}n^{b,2}\|_{Z^{1,{m}}_{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|n^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s+1}^{1,m}}^{2}+C\|c^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}\|\partial_{z}n^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\left(\|n^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}+\|\partial_{z}n^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\partial^{2}_{z}n^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)\|\partial_{z}c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\left(\|n^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}n^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)\|\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}.

(d) For the
c^{b,2}
and
u_{1}^{b,2}
, we derive

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\left(\|c^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}+\|u_{1}^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}\right)+(1-C\sigma-C\delta)\left(\|\partial_{z}c^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\delta}\left(1+\|u^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}+\|c^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+2}}+\|n^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+2}}\right)^{2}\|c^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\delta}\left(1+\|u^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}+\|c^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}\right)\|u_{1}^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}+g_{1},

where

\displaystyle g_{1}=

\displaystyle C_{\delta}\left(\|n^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}+\|u^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}+\|(u_{1}^{b,1},n^{b,1})\|_{Z_{s+2}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|(\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1},\partial_{z}n^{b,1})\|_{Z_{0}^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\left(1+\|c^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}+\|c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s+1}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s+3}^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\left(1+\|u^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}+\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{2}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)\|(u_{1}^{b,1},\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1},n^{b,1})\|_{Z_{s+2}^{1,m+1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\|(\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1},\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1})\|_{Z_{0}^{1,m+1}}^{2}\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s+2}^{1,m+1}}^{2}.

(e) For the
\partial_{z}c^{b,2}
and
\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,2}
, we have the following energy inequality:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\left(\|\partial_{z}c^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}\right)+(1-C\sigma-C\delta)\left(\|\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\delta}\left(1+\|u^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}+\|c^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}+\|n^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+2}}\right)^{2}\|\partial_{z}c^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\delta}\left(1+\|u^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}+\|c^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}\right)\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,2}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m}}^{2}+g_{2},

where

\displaystyle g_{2}=

\displaystyle C_{\delta}\bigg(\|n^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}+\|u^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}+\|(u_{1}^{b,1},n^{b,1})\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|(\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1},\partial_{z}n^{b,1})\|_{Z_{s+1}^{1,m+1}}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\times\left(1+\|c^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}+\|c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s+1}^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\left(\|u^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}+\|(\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1},u_{1}^{b,1})\|_{Z_{0}^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)\left(\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s+1}^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C\left(\|u^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}+\|c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s}^{1,m+1}}^{2}+\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s+2}^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s+2}^{1,m+1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\delta}\left(\|u^{e,0}\|_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}^{2}+\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s+2}^{1,m+1}}^{2}\right)\|\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{s+2}^{1,m+1}}^{2}.

MM

\square

Proof of Lemma
6.6
.

We divide it into two parts:

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}((u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}G),(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle(u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\widehat{\nabla}G,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|

\displaystyle+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha,|\beta|\leq m-1}\left|\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha-\beta}(u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\partial}^{\beta}\widehat{\nabla}G,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle K_{1}+K_{2}.

For
K_{1}
, swapping
\partial_{z}
and
\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle(u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\widehat{\nabla}G,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle(u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle u_{2}^{E,1}[\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha},\partial_{z}]G,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|,

and by (
6.125
), (
4.38
), (
4.63
) and integration by parts, this becomes

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}(1+z)^{2s}(u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}(\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G)^{2}dxdy\right|

\displaystyle+C\delta\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle z\overline{\partial_{y}u_{2}^{e,0}}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha-(0,0,1)}\partial_{z}G,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2s\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}(1+z)^{2s-1}z\overline{\partial_{y}u_{2}^{e,0}}(\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G)^{2}dxdy\right|+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\overline{\partial_{y}u_{2}^{e,0}}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left\|\overline{\partial_{y}u_{2}^{e,0}}\right\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\|(1+z)^{s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\|_{2}^{2}.

(6.157)

For
K_{2}
,
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha,|\beta|\leq m-1}\left|\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha-\beta}(u^{E,0})_{p}\cdot\widehat{\partial}^{\beta}\widehat{\nabla}G,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha,|\beta|\leq m-1}\left|\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha-\beta}u_{1}^{E,0}\widehat{\partial}^{\beta}\partial_{x}G,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|

\displaystyle+C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha,|\beta|\leq m-1}\left|\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha-\beta}u_{2}^{E,1}\widehat{\partial}^{\beta}\partial_{z}G,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|,

then by (
4.38
) and (
6.124
), it can be controlled by

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha,|\beta|\leq m-1}\left|\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha-\beta}\overline{u_{1}^{e,0}}\widehat{\partial}^{\beta+(0,1,0)}G,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C}{\delta}\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha,|\beta|\leq m-1}\left|\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha-\beta}\overline{\partial_{y}u_{2}^{e,0}}\widehat{\partial}^{\beta+(0,0,1)}G,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha,|\beta|\leq m-1}\left|\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha-\beta-(0,0,1)}\overline{\partial_{y}u_{2}^{e,0}}\widehat{\partial}^{\beta+(0,0,1)}G,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}G\rangle\right|.

Finally, by Hölder inequality and the embedding property, we have

\displaystyle K_{2}\leq C\left(\left\|u_{1}^{e,0}\right\|_{\overline{H}^{l,m+2}}+\left\|\partial_{y}u_{2}^{e,0}\right\|_{\overline{H}^{l,m+2}}\right)\|G\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}^{2}.

(6.158)

Combining (
6.2
) and (
6.158
), this lemma can be proven.

MM

\square

Proof of Lemma
6.7
.

(a) By the Leibniz rule and Hölder inequality, it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}(HG),(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}Q\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\|\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha-\beta}H\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\|(1+z)^{s}\widehat{\partial}^{\beta}G\|_{2}\|(1+z)^{s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}Q\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\|\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha-\beta}H\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}^{2}\|(1+z)^{s}\widehat{\partial}^{\beta}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\|(1+z)^{s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}Q\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,\alpha_{3}=0,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}H\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\|G\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}\|Q\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}.

(b) By Lemma
5.2
, it shows that

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\left|\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}(HG),(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}Q\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\|\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha-\beta}H\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|(1+z)^{s}\widehat{\partial}^{\beta}G\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\|(1+z)^{s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}Q\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|H\|_{Z_{0}^{l,m+1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{z}H\|_{Z_{0}^{l,m+1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|G\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}\|Q\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}.

MM

\square

Proof of Lemma
6.8
.

Firstly, by integration by parts we have

\displaystyle-\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}^{2}H,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}H\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}H,\partial_{z}\{(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}H\}\rangle-\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle[\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha},\partial_{z}]\partial_{z}H,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}H\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2s\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}H,(1+z)^{2s-1}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}H\rangle+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}H,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}H\rangle

\displaystyle-\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle[\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha},\partial_{z}]\partial_{z}H,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}H\rangle+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}H,(1+z)^{2s}[\partial_{z},\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}]H\rangle.

Finally, by (
6.125
) and Hölder inequality, we derive

\displaystyle D

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\|(1+z)^{s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}H\|_{2}^{2}-2s\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}H,(1+z)^{2s-1}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}H\rangle|

\displaystyle\qquad-C\delta\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}|\langle\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}H,(1+z)^{2s}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha-(0,0,1)}\partial_{z}H\rangle|

\displaystyle\geq\|\partial_{z}H\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}^{2}-C\left(\sigma\|\partial_{z}H\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\sigma}\|H\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s-1}}^{2}\right)-C\delta\left(\|\partial_{z}H\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}^{2}+\|\partial_{z}H\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s-1}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq(1-C\sigma-C\delta)\|\partial_{z}H\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s}}^{2}-\frac{C}{\sigma}\|H\|_{Z^{l,m}_{s-1}}^{2}.

MM

\square

6.3.
Proof of Proposition
2.1

In this section, we establish the uniform boundedness in the conormal Sobolev spaces of the approximate solutions and remainders
N,K,U
.

First, concluding the results of Proposition
6.1
, Proposition
6.4
, and Proposition
6.5
, we obtain the following estimates immediately.

Proposition 6.9
.

Assume that
(n^{e,0},c^{e,0},u^{e,0})
are the same as in Proposition
6.1
,
(n^{b,1},c^{b,1},u^{b,1})
are the same as in Proposition
6.4
, and
(n^{b,2},c^{b,2},u^{b,2})
are the same as in Proposition
6.5
. Then we have

(a)

\displaystyle\|n^{a}\|_{Y^{1,3}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|c^{a}\|_{Y^{1,4}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|u^{a}\|_{Y^{1,4}_{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Bigl(\|n^{e,0}\|_{Y^{1,3}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|(c^{e,0},u^{e,0})\|_{Y^{1,4}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|(n^{b,1},n^{b,2},\partial_{z}n^{b,1},\partial_{z}n^{b,2})\|_{Z^{1,4}_{0}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|(c^{b,1},c^{b,2},\partial_{z}c^{b,1},\partial_{z}c^{b,2},u_{1}^{b,1},\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1},u_{2}^{b,2},\partial_{z}u_{2}^{b,2})\|_{Z^{1,5}_{0}}^{2}\Bigr),

(b)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}n^{a}\|_{Y^{1,2}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}c^{a}\|_{Y^{1,3}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}u^{a}\|_{Y^{1,3}_{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Bigl(\|\partial_{y}n^{e,0}\|_{Y^{1,2}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|(\partial_{y}c^{e,0},\partial_{y}u^{e,0})\|_{Y^{1,3}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|(\partial_{z}n^{b,1},\partial_{z}n^{b,2},\partial_{z}^{2}n^{b,1},\partial_{z}^{2}n^{b,2})\|_{Z^{1,3}_{0}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|(\partial_{z}c^{b,j},\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,j},\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1},\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1},\partial_{z}u_{2}^{b,2},\partial_{z}^{2}u_{2}^{b,2})\|_{Z^{1,4}_{0}}^{2}\Bigr),

(c)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}c^{a}\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}u^{a}\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Bigl(\|(\partial_{y}^{2}c^{e,0},\partial_{y}^{2}u^{e,0})\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1},\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,2},\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1},\partial_{z}^{2}u_{2}^{b,2})\|_{Z^{1,2}}^{2}\Bigr).

Proof.

For (a), by (
2.20
) and embedding theory, we have

\displaystyle\|n^{a}\|_{Y^{1,3}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|c^{a}\|_{Y^{1,4}_{\infty}}^{2}+\|u^{a}\|_{Y^{1,4}_{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big(\|(n^{e,0},\varepsilon n^{b,1},\varepsilon^{2}n^{b,2)}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,3}}^{2}+\|(c^{e,0},\varepsilon c^{b,1},\varepsilon^{2}c^{b,2})\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,4}}^{2}+\|(u^{e,0},\varepsilon u_{1}^{b,1},\varepsilon^{2}u_{2}^{b,2})\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,4}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big(\|n^{e,0}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,3}}^{2}+\|c^{e,0}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,4}}^{2}+\|u^{e,0}\|_{Y_{\infty}^{1,4}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon^{2}\Big(\|n^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,4}}\|\partial_{z}n^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,4}}+\|c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,5}}\|\partial_{z}c^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,5}}+\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,5}}\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,5}}\Big)

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon^{4}\Big(\|n^{b,2}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,4}}\|\partial_{z}n^{b,2}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,4}}+\|c^{b,2}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,5}}\|\partial_{z}c^{b,2}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,5}}+\|u_{2}^{b,2}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,5}}\|\partial_{z}u_{2}^{b,2}\|_{Z_{0}^{1,5}}\Big),

where we have used

\displaystyle\|h(t,x,z)\|_{Y^{l,m}_{\infty}}^{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}h\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m}\left\|\frac{\psi}{\delta y}\right\|^{2\alpha_{3}}_{\infty}\|\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}h\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq l,|\alpha|\leq m+1}\|\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}h\|_{L_{xz}^{2}}\|\partial_{z}\widehat{\partial}^{\alpha}h\|_{L_{xz}^{2}},

then the proof of (a) is completed.

For (b), note that

\displaystyle\partial_{y}n^{a}=\partial_{y}n^{e,0}+\partial_{z}n^{b,1}+\varepsilon\partial_{z}^{2}n^{b,2},

\displaystyle\partial_{y}c^{a}=\partial_{y}c^{e,0}+\partial_{z}c^{b,1}+\varepsilon\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,2},

\displaystyle\partial_{y}u^{a}=\partial_{y}u^{e,0}+\binom{\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}}{\varepsilon\partial_{z}^{2}u_{2}^{b,2}},

then the proof is similar to (a).

For (c), note that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}c^{a}\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}u^{a}\|_{Y^{1,2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{y}^{2}c^{e,0},\partial_{y}^{2}u^{e,0})\|_{Y^{l,2}}^{2}+\|(\varepsilon^{-1}\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1},\varepsilon^{-1}\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1})\|_{Y^{l,2}}^{2}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1},\partial_{z}^{2}u_{2}^{b,2})\|_{Y^{l,2}}^{2},

then by (
6.128
), this becomes

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{y}^{2}c^{e,0},\partial_{y}^{2}u^{e,0})\|_{Y^{l,2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon\|(\varepsilon^{-1}\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1},\varepsilon^{-1}\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1})\|_{Z_{0}^{l,2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon\|(\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1},\partial_{z}^{2}u_{2}^{b,2})\|_{Z_{0}^{l,2}}^{2},

and the proof is complete.

MM

\square

Second, for the remainders
N,K,U
there hold the following inequalities.

Proposition 6.10
.

Assume that
(n^{e,0},c^{e,0},u^{e,0})
are the same as in Proposition
6.1
,
(n^{b,1},c^{b,1},u^{b,1})
are the same as in Proposition
6.4
, and
(n^{b,2},c^{b,2},u^{b,2})
are the same as in Proposition
6.5
. Then we have

\displaystyle\|N\|^{2}_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{3}\|(n^{b,1},n^{b,2})\|_{Z^{2,m+1}_{0}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{3}\Big(1+\|c^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}+\|u^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}+\big\|u_{1}^{b,1}\big\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|(c^{b,1},c^{b,2},\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{0}}+\|\partial_{z}c^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{1}}+\|\partial_{z}c^{b,2}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m}_{0}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big(\|n^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}+\|(n^{b,1},n^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{0}}+\|(\partial_{z}n^{b,1},\partial_{z}n^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{1}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{3}\Big(\|n^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}+\|\partial_{z}c^{b,2}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m}_{0}}+\|(n^{b,2},\partial_{z}n^{b,2},n^{b,1},\partial_{z}n^{b,1})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{0}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big(\|\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{2}}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,2}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m}_{1}}+\|(\partial_{z}^{2}n^{b,1},\partial_{z}^{2}n^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{0}}\Big),

\displaystyle\|K\|^{2}_{Y^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{5}\Big(1+\|(u_{1}^{e,0},n^{e,0})\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,4}}\Big)\|(c^{b,1},c^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,3}_{1}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|u_{2}^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,5}}\|(\partial_{z}c^{b,1},\partial_{z}c^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,0}_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{4}\|c^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,5}}\Big(1+\|(n^{b,1},n^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,0}_{1}}+\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,2}_{3}}+\|u_{1}^{b,2}\|^{2}_{Z_{0}^{1,0}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{7}\|(u_{1}^{b,1},\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1},n^{b,1},\partial_{z}n^{b,1},n^{b,2},\partial_{z}n^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,2}_{0}}\|c^{b,2}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,2}_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{5}\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,3}_{2}}\|(\partial_{z}c^{b,2},\partial_{z}c^{b,1})\|^{2}_{Z_{0}^{1,0}}+\varepsilon^{7}\|(c^{b,1},\partial_{z}c^{b,1})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,2}_{0}}\|n^{b,2}\|^{2}_{Z_{0}^{1,0}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}K\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{5}\Big(1+\|(u_{1}^{e,0},n^{e,0})\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}\Big)\|(c^{b,1},c^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+3}_{1}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|u_{2}^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+5}}\|(\partial_{z}c^{b,1},\partial_{z}c^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{4}\|c^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+5}}\Big(1+\|(n^{b,1},n^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{1}}+\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{3}}+\|u_{1}^{b,2}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{0}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{7}\|(u_{1}^{b,1},\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1},n^{b,1},\partial_{z}n^{b,1},n^{b,2},\partial_{z}n^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{0}}\|c^{b,2}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{5}\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+3}_{2}}\|(\partial_{z}c^{b,1},\partial_{z}c^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{0}}+\varepsilon^{7}\|(c^{b,1},\partial_{z}c^{b,1})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{0}}\|n^{b,2}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{0}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}K\|^{2}_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{3}\Big(1+\|u^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}+\|n^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+3}}\Big)\Big(\|(c^{b,1},c^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{0}}+\|(\partial_{z}c^{b,1},\partial_{z}c^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{1}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{3}\|c^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}\Big(1+\|(n^{b,1},n^{b,2},\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m}_{0}}+\|(\partial_{z}n^{b,1},\partial_{z}n^{b,2},\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m}_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{2}}\Big)+\varepsilon^{5}\|(\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1},\partial_{z}^{2}n^{b,1})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{0}}\|c^{b,2}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{5}\|(u_{1}^{b,1},\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1},n^{b,1},\partial_{z}n^{b,1},n^{b,2},\partial_{z}n^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{0}}\|(c^{b,2},\partial_{z}c^{b,2},c^{b,1},\partial_{z}c^{b,1})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{3}\Big(\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{2}}+\|n^{b,2}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m}_{0}}+\|u_{2}^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}\Big)\|(\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,1},\partial_{z}^{2}c^{b,2})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{2}},

\displaystyle\|U_{1}\|^{2}_{Y^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{4}\|u_{1}^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,5}}\Big(1+\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,2}_{2}}\Big)+\varepsilon^{5}\|u_{2}^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,5}}\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,1}_{2}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|n^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,2}_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{5}\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,3}_{0}}\Big(1+\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,3}_{0}}\Big)+\varepsilon^{5}\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,2}_{2}}\left(\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,2}_{0}}+\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,2}_{0}}\right),

\displaystyle\|U_{2}\|^{2}_{Y^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{5}\big\|u_{1}^{b,1}\big\|^{2}_{Z^{2,3}_{2}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|n^{b,2}\|^{2}_{Y^{1}}+\varepsilon^{4}\|u^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,5}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z^{1,2}_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{4}\Big(1+\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,3}_{0}}+\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,2}_{0}}+\|u^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,5}}\Big)\big\|u_{1}^{b,1}\big\|^{2}_{Z^{1,4}_{2}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}U_{1}\|_{Y^{1,m}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{3}\|u^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}\Big(1+\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{1}}+\big\|u_{1}^{b,1}\big\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{2}}\Big)+\varepsilon^{3}\|n^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{3}\Big(1+\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{0}}\Big)\|(u_{1}^{b,1},\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1})\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{3}\left(\|u_{2}^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+4}}+\big\|u_{1}^{b,1}\big\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{2}}\right)\|\partial_{z}^{2}u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{2}},

and

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}U_{2}\|^{2}_{Y^{1,m}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{5}\big\|u_{1}^{b,1}\big\|^{2}_{Z^{2,m+3}_{2}}+\varepsilon^{4}\|u_{2}^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+5}}\Big(1+\big\|u_{1}^{b,1}\big\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{5}\Big(1+\|u_{1}^{e,0}\|^{2}_{\overline{H}^{1,m+5}}+\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{0}}+\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+3}_{0}}\Big)\big\|u_{1}^{b,1}\big\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+4}_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{5}\|n^{b,2}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+1}_{0}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\partial_{z}u_{1}^{b,1}\|^{2}_{Z^{1,m+2}_{0}}.

Proposition 6.11
.

Assume that
(n^{e,0},c^{e,0},u^{e,0})
are the same as in Proposition
6.1
,
(n^{b,1},c^{b,1},u^{b,1})
are the same as in Proposition
6.4
, and
(n^{b,2},c^{b,2},u^{b,2})
are the same as in Proposition
6.5
. Then we have

\displaystyle\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{2,{5}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z^{2,6}_{1}},

and

\displaystyle\|Fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{2,2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z^{2,2}_{1}}.

Proof.

By direct computations, we have

\displaystyle\|fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{2,{5}}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 5,|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\left|\partial^{\alpha}\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{x}u_{1}^{b,1}dze^{-y}\right)\right|^{2}dxdy

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 5,|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\left(1+z\right)^{2}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x}u_{1}^{b,1}\right)^{2}dxdz\leq C\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z^{2,6}_{1}}^{2},

and

\displaystyle\|Fe^{-y}\|_{Y^{2,2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2,|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\left|\partial^{\alpha}\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}u_{1}^{b,1}dze^{-y}\right)\right|^{2}dxdy

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2,|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\left(1+z\right)^{2}\left(\partial^{\alpha}u_{1}^{b,1}\right)^{2}dxdz\leq\|u_{1}^{b,1}\|_{Z^{2,2}_{1}}^{2},

where the definition of
f
in (
4.71
). The proof is complete.

MM

\square

Proof of Proposition
2.1
.

Proof.

It follows from the above three propositions, together with integration in
t
, that this proof is complete.
MM

\square
